  Maven Plugin – japicmp-base     [Fork me on GitHub](https://github.com/siom79/japicmp)\n\n---\n\n-   [japicmp](https://siom79.github.io/japicmp)/\n-   Maven Plugin\n-   | Last Published:   [Release Notes](ReleaseNotes.html)\n\n[![Built by Maven](./images/logos/maven-feather.png)](https://maven.apache.org/)\n\n# Maven Plugin\n\n## Basic Usage\n\nThe maven plugin can be included in the pom.xml file of your artifact in the following way (requires maven >= 3.0.3):\n\n```xml\n<plugin>\n\t<groupId>com.github.siom79.japicmp</groupId>\n\t<artifactId>japicmp-maven-plugin</artifactId>\n\t<version>0.26.1</version>\n\t<configuration>\n\t\t<oldVersion>\n\t\t\t<dependency>\n\t\t\t\t<groupId>${project.groupId}</groupId>\n\t\t\t\t<artifactId>${project.artifactId}</artifactId>\n\t\t\t\t<version>${oldversion}</version>\n\t\t\t\t<type>jar</type>\n\t\t\t</dependency>\n\t\t</oldVersion>\n\t\t<newVersion>\n\t\t\t<file>\n\t\t\t\t<path>${project.build.directory}/${project.artifactId}-${project.version}.${project.packaging}</path>\n\t\t\t</file>\n\t\t</newVersion>\n\t\t<parameter>\n\t\t\t<!-- see documentation -->\n\t\t</parameter>\n\t</configuration>\n\t<executions>\n\t\t<execution>\n\t\t\t<phase>verify</phase>\n\t\t\t<goals>\n\t\t\t\t<goal>cmp</goal>\n\t\t\t</goals>\n\t\t</execution>\n\t</executions>\n</plugin>\n```\n\nYou can also leave out the <oldVersion> and <newVersion> elements:\n\n```xml\n<plugin>\n\t<groupId>com.github.siom79.japicmp</groupId>\n\t<artifactId>japicmp-maven-plugin</artifactId>\n\t<version>0.26.1</version>\n\t<configuration>\n\t\t<parameter>\n\t\t\t<!-- see documentation -->\n\t\t</parameter>\n\t</configuration>\n\t<executions>\n\t\t<execution>\n\t\t\t<phase>verify</phase>\n\t\t\t<goals>\n\t\t\t\t<goal>cmp</goal>\n\t\t\t</goals>\n\t\t</execution>\n\t</executions>\n</plugin>\n```\n\nThis way the plugin tries to determine the artifacts output (new version) and the latest released version (no SNAPSHOT version). If you need to configure the latest version more precisely (e.g. only GA versions), then you can use the parameter <oldVersionPattern>:\n\n```xml\n<plugin>\n\t<groupId>com.github.siom79.japicmp</groupId>\n\t<artifactId>japicmp-maven-plugin</artifactId>\n\t<version>0.26.1</version>\n\t<configuration>\n\t\t<parameter>\n        \t<oldVersionPattern>\\d+\\.\\d+\\.\\d+\\.GA</oldVersionPattern>\n        </parameter>\n\t</configuration>\n\t<executions>\n\t\t<execution>\n\t\t\t<phase>verify</phase>\n\t\t\t<goals>\n\t\t\t\t<goal>cmp</goal>\n\t\t\t</goals>\n\t\t</execution>\n\t</executions>\n</plugin>\n```\n\n## Properties\n\nThe following properties can be set:\n\nProperty\n\nDescription\n\njapicmp.skip\n\nSkip the execution of this plugin.\n\njapicmp.skipXmlReport\n\nSkip the generation of the XML report.\n\njapicmp.skipHtmlReport\n\nSkip the generation of the HTML report.\n\njapicmp.skipMarkdownReport\n\nSkip the generation of the Markdown report.\n\njapicmp.breakBuildOnModifications\n\nBreak the build in case of any modifications.\n\njapicmp.breakBuildOnBinaryIncompatibleModifications\n\nBreak the build in case of any binary incompatible modifications.\n\njapicmp.breakBuildOnSourceIncompatibleModifications\n\nBreak the build in case of any source incompatible modifications.\n\njapicmp.breakBuildBasedOnSemanticVersioning\n\nBreak the build in case the semantic versioning is violated.\n\njapicmp.breakBuildBasedOnSemanticVersioningForMajorVersionZero\n\nBreak the build in case of semantic versioning used with zero as major version number is violated.\n\n## Advanced Usage\n\nAn advanced configuration can utilize the following parameters:\n\n```xml\n<build>\n\t<plugins>\n\t\t<plugin>\n\t\t\t<groupId>com.github.siom79.japicmp</groupId>\n\t\t\t<artifactId>japicmp-maven-plugin</artifactId>\n\t\t\t<version>0.26.1</version>\n\t\t\t<configuration>\n\t\t\t\t<oldVersion>\n\t\t\t\t\t<dependency>\n\t\t\t\t\t\t<groupId>japicmp</groupId>\n\t\t\t\t\t\t<artifactId>japicmp-test-v1</artifactId>\n\t\t\t\t\t\t<version>0.26.1</version>\n\t\t\t\t\t\t<type>jar</type>\n\t\t\t\t\t</dependency>\n\t\t\t\t</oldVersion>\n\t\t\t\t<newVersion>\n\t\t\t\t\t<file>\n\t\t\t\t\t\t<path>${project.build.directory}/${project.artifactId}-${project.version}.${project.packaging}</path>\n\t\t\t\t\t</file>\n\t\t\t\t</newVersion>\n\t\t\t\t<parameter>\n\t\t\t\t\t<onlyModified>true</onlyModified>\n\t\t\t\t\t<includes>\n\t\t\t\t\t\t<include>package.to.include</include>\n\t\t\t\t\t\t<include>package.ClassToInclude</include>\n\t\t\t\t\t\t<include>package.Class#methodToInclude(long,int)</include>\n\t\t\t\t\t\t<include>package.Class#fieldToInclude</include>\n\t\t\t\t\t\t<include>@my.AnnotationToInclude</include>\n\t\t\t\t\t</includes>\n\t\t\t\t\t<excludes>\n\t\t\t\t\t\t<exclude>package.to.exclude</exclude>\n\t\t\t\t\t\t<exclude>package.ClassToExclude</exclude>\n\t\t\t\t\t\t<exclude>package.Class#methodToExclude(long,int)</exclude>\n\t\t\t\t\t\t<exclude>package.Class#fieldToExclude</exclude>\n\t\t\t\t\t\t<exclude>@my.AnnotationToExclude</exclude>\n\t\t\t\t\t</excludes>\n\t\t\t\t\t<includeExclusively>false</includeExclusively>\n\t\t\t\t\t<excludeExclusively>false</excludeExclusively>\n\t\t\t\t\t<accessModifier>public</accessModifier>\n\t\t\t\t\t<breakBuildOnModifications>false</breakBuildOnModifications>\n\t\t\t\t\t<breakBuildOnBinaryIncompatibleModifications>false</breakBuildOnBinaryIncompatibleModifications>\n\t\t\t\t\t<breakBuildOnSourceIncompatibleModifications>false</breakBuildOnSourceIncompatibleModifications>\n\t\t\t\t\t<breakBuildBasedOnSemanticVersioning>false</breakBuildBasedOnSemanticVersioning>\n\t\t\t\t\t<breakBuildBasedOnSemanticVersioningForMajorVersionZero>false</breakBuildBasedOnSemanticVersioningForMajorVersionZero>\n\t\t\t\t\t<breakBuildIfCausedByExclusion>true</breakBuildIfCausedByExclusion>\n\t\t\t\t\t<overrideCompatibilityChangeParameters>\n\t\t\t\t\t\t<overrideCompatibilityChangeParameter>\n\t\t\t\t\t\t\t<compatibilityChange>CLASS_REMOVED</compatibilityChange>\n\t\t\t\t\t\t\t<binaryCompatible>true</binaryCompatible>\n\t\t\t\t\t\t\t<sourceCompatible>true</sourceCompatible>\n\t\t\t\t\t\t\t<semanticVersionLevel>PATCH</semanticVersionLevel>\n\t\t\t\t\t\t</overrideCompatibilityChangeParameter>\n\t\t\t\t\t</overrideCompatibilityChangeParameters>\n\t\t\t\t\t<onlyBinaryIncompatible>false</onlyBinaryIncompatible>\n\t\t\t\t\t<includeSynthetic>false</includeSynthetic>\n\t\t\t\t\t<ignoreMissingClasses>false</ignoreMissingClasses>\n\t\t\t\t\t<ignoreMissingClassesByRegularExpressions>\n\t\t\t\t\t\t<ignoreMissingClassesByRegularExpression>org.apache.commons.math3.*</ignoreMissingClassesByRegularExpression>\n\t\t\t\t\t</ignoreMissingClassesByRegularExpressions>\n\t\t\t\t\t<skipPomModules>true</skipPomModules>\n\t\t\t\t\t<htmlStylesheet>path/to/stylesheet.css</htmlStylesheet>\n\t\t\t\t\t<htmlTitle>Title of report</htmlTitle>\n\t\t\t\t\t<markdownTitle>Title of report</markdownTitle>\n\t\t\t\t\t<noAnnotations>false</noAnnotations>\n\t\t\t\t\t<ignoreNonResolvableArtifacts>false</ignoreNonResolvableArtifacts>\n\t\t\t\t\t<ignoreMissingOptionalDependency>false</ignoreMissingOptionalDependency>\n\t\t\t\t\t<packagingSupporteds>\n\t\t\t\t\t\t<packagingSupported>jar</packagingSupported>\n\t\t\t\t\t</packagingSupporteds>\n\t\t\t\t\t<postAnalysisScript>${project.basedir}/src/main/groovy/postAnalysisScript.groovy</postAnalysisScript>\n\t\t\t\t\t<reportOnlyFilename>false</reportOnlyFilename>\n\t\t\t\t\t<skipXmlReport>false</skipXmlReport>\n\t\t\t\t\t<skipHtmlReport>false</skipHtmlReport>\n\t\t\t\t\t<skipMarkdownReport>false</skipMarkdownReport>\n\t\t\t\t\t<skipDiffReport>false</skipDiffReport>\n\t\t\t\t\t<includeModules>\n\t\t\t\t\t\t<includeModule>.*incl.*</includeModule>\n\t\t\t\t\t</includeModules>\n\t\t\t\t\t<excludeModules>\n\t\t\t\t\t\t<excludeModule>.*incl.*</excludeModule>\n\t\t\t\t\t</excludeModules>\n\t\t\t\t\t<reportLinkName>artifact1_vs_artifact2</reportLinkName>\n\t\t\t\t</parameter>\n\t\t\t\t<dependencies>\n\t\t\t\t\t<dependency>\n\t\t\t\t\t\t<groupId>org.apache.commons</groupId>\n\t\t\t\t\t\t<artifactId>commons-math3</artifactId>\n\t\t\t\t\t\t<version>3.4</version>\n\t\t\t\t\t</dependency>\n\t\t\t\t</dependencies>\n\t\t\t\t<skip>false</skip>\n\t\t\t</configuration>\n\t\t\t<executions>\n\t\t\t\t<execution>\n\t\t\t\t\t<phase>verify</phase>\n\t\t\t\t\t<goals>\n\t\t\t\t\t\t<goal>cmp</goal>\n\t\t\t\t\t</goals>\n\t\t\t\t</execution>\n\t\t\t</executions>\n\t\t</plugin>\n\t</plugins>\n</build>\n```\n\nThe elements <oldVersion> and <newVersion> elements let you specify which version you want to compare. Both elements support either a <dependency> or a <file> element. If necessary you can select the artifact by providing a <classifier> element inside the <dependency> element. Through the <parameter> element you can provide the following options:\n\nParameter\n\nOptional\n\nDefault\n\nDescription\n\nbreakBuildOnModifications\n\ntrue\n\nfalse\n\nIf set to true, the build breaks in case a modification has been detected.\n\nbreakBuildOnBinaryIncompatibleModifications\n\ntrue\n\nfalse\n\nIf set to true, the build breaks in case a binary incompatible modification has been detected.\n\nbreakBuildOnSourceIncompatibleModifications\n\ntrue\n\nfalse\n\nIf set to true, the build breaks in case a source incompatible modification has been detected.\n\nbreakBuildBasedOnSemanticVersioning\n\ntrue\n\nfalse\n\nIf set to true, the plugin analyzes the versions of the old and new archives and decides based on these versions if binary compatible or incompatible changes are allowed or not. This option expects versions in the form Major.Minor.Patch (e.g. 1.2.3 or 1.2.3-SNAPSHOT).\n\nbreakBuildBasedOnSemanticVersioningForMajorVersionZero\n\ntrue\n\nfalse\n\nIf set to true, the plugin will apply the option breakBuildBasedOnSemanticVersioning also for projects with major version zero (which is not required by the Semantic Versioning Specification).\n\nbreakBuildIfCausedByExclusion\n\ntrue\n\ntrue\n\nIf set to false, the plugin won't break the build if the incompatible change was caused by an excluded class (e.g. excluded interface removed from not excluded class).\n\noverrideCompatibilityChangeParameters\n\ntrue\n\nn.a.\n\nCan be used to override default values for binary and source compatibility checks.\n\nonlyBinaryIncompatible\n\ntrue\n\nfalse\n\nIf set to true, only binary incompatible changes are reported.\n\nonlyModified\n\ntrue\n\nfalse\n\nOutputs only modified classes/methods. If not set to true, all classes and methods are printed.\n\nincludeSynthetic\n\ntrue\n\nfalse\n\nIf set to true, changes for synthetic classes and class members are tracked.\n\nnoAnnotations\n\ntrue\n\nfalse\n\nSetting this option to true disables the evaluation of annotations completely.\n\nreportOnlyFilename\n\ntrue\n\nfalse\n\nIf set to true, report will include only the artifact filename, not the complete artifact file path.\n\nreportOnlySummary\n\ntrue\n\nfalse\n\nIf set to true, report only a breakdown of classes and their status when generating plain text or html reports.\n\nignoreMissingClasses\n\ntrue\n\nfalse\n\nIf set to true, superclasses and interfaces that cannot be resolved are ignored. Pleases note that in this case the results for the affected classes may not be accurate.\n\nignoreMissingClassesByRegularExpressions\n\ntrue\n\nn.a.\n\nList of regular expressions for superclasses and interfaces that cannot be resolved and that should be ignored. In contrast to the option ignoreMissingClasses, which ignores all missing classes, this options allows a fine-grained selection.\n\naccessModifier\n\ntrue\n\nprotected\n\nSets the access modifier level (public, protected, package\\_protected, private).\n\nincludes\n\ntrue\n\nn.a.\n\nList of package, classes, methods and field that should be included. The syntax is similar to the one used for javadoc references. Annotations can also be used for filtering, just let the fully qualified name start with @.\n\nexcludes\n\ntrue\n\nn.a.\n\nList of package, classes, methods and field that should be excluded. The syntax is similar to the one used for javadoc references. Annotations can also be used for filtering, just let the fully qualified name start with @.\n\nincludeExclusively\n\ntrue\n\nfalse\n\nInclude only packages specified in the “includes” parameter, exclude their sub-packages.\n\nexcludeExclusively\n\ntrue\n\nfalse\n\nExclude only packages specified in the “excludes” parameter, include their sub-packages.\n\nhtmlStylesheet\n\ntrue\n\nn.a.\n\nPath to an individual CSS stylesheet for the HTML report.\n\nhtmlTitle\n\ntrue\n\nn.a.\n\nA title for the HTML report (optional).\n\nmarkdownTitle\n\ntrue\n\nn.a.\n\nA title for the Markdown report (optional).\n\nskipPomModules\n\ntrue\n\ntrue\n\nSetting this parameter to false (default: true) will not skip execution in modules with packaging type pom.\n\nskip\n\ntrue\n\nfalse\n\nSetting this parameter to true will skip execution of the plugin.\n\nignoreNonResolvableArtifacts\n\ntrue\n\nfalse\n\nSet this to true in order to ignore artifacts that cannot be resolved, i.e. the build does not break in case a dependency cannot be resolved to a file.\n\nignoreMissingOptionalDependency\n\ntrue\n\nfalse\n\nIgnore missing optional dependencies.\n\nignoreMissingOldVersion\n\ntrue\n\nfalse\n\nIf set to true, not resolvable artifacts for the old version do not break the build.\n\nignoreMissingNewVersion\n\ntrue\n\nfalse\n\nIf set to true, not resolvable artifacts for the new version do not break the build.\n\npackagingSupported\n\ntrue\n\nn.a.\n\nList all packaging type for which the plugin should be executed. Helpful if you define the plugin in a root pom.\n\npostAnalysisScript\n\ntrue\n\nn.a.\n\nA [Groovy](https://www.groovy-lang.org/) script that gets invoked after analysis is completed and before the output is written. your library implements interfaces or extends classes from other libraries than the JDK, you can add these dependencies by using the <dependencies> element:\n\n```xml\n<dependencies>\n\t<dependency>\n\t\t<groupId>org.apache.commons</groupId>\n\t\t<artifactId>commons-math3</artifactId>\n\t\t<version>3.4</version>\n\t</dependency>\n</dependencies>\n```\n\nDependencies declared in the enclosing pom.xml and its parents are added automatically. The dependencies declared explicitly for this plugin are appended to the classpath before the ones from the enclosing pom.xml, hence you can override them.\n\nIn case the classpath between both versions differs, you can add the dependencies for the new and old version separately:\n\n```xml\n<oldClassPathDependencies>\n\t<dependency>\n\t\t<groupId>org.apache.commons</groupId>\n\t\t<artifactId>commons-math3</artifactId>\n\t\t<version>3.4</version>\n\t</dependency>\n</oldClassPathDependencies>\n<newClassPathDependencies>\n\t<dependency>\n\t\t<groupId>org.apache.commons</groupId>\n\t\t<artifactId>commons-math3</artifactId>\n\t\t<version>3.5</version>\n\t</dependency>\n</newClassPathDependencies>\n```\n\nThe maven plugin produces the two files `japicmp.diff` and `japicmp.xml` within the directory `${project.build.directory}/japicmp` of your artifact. If you run the plugin multiple times within the same module using the <executions> element, the reports are named after the execution id.\n\n## Site report\n\nAlternatively it can be used inside the `<reporting/>` tag in order to be invoked by the [maven-site-plugin](https://maven.apache.org/plugins/maven-site-plugin/) and therewith to be integrated into the site report:\n\n```xml\n<reporting>\n\t<plugins>\n\t\t<plugin>\n\t\t\t<groupId>com.github.siom79.japicmp</groupId>\n\t\t\t<artifactId>japicmp-maven-plugin</artifactId>\n\t\t\t<version>0.26.1</version>\n\t\t\t<reportSets>\n\t\t\t\t<reportSet>\n\t\t\t\t\t<reports>\n\t\t\t\t\t\t<report>cmp-report</report>\n\t\t\t\t\t</reports>\n\t\t\t\t</reportSet>\n\t\t\t</reportSets>\n\t\t\t<configuration>\n\t\t\t\t<!-- see above -->\n\t\t\t</configuration>\n\t\t</plugin>\n\t</plugins>\n</reporting>\n```\n\nTo create a summary report, you can also provide multiple old and new versions:\n\n```xml\n<configuration>\n\t<oldVersions>\n\t\t<dependency>\n\t\t\t<groupId>com.github.siom79.japicmp</groupId>\n\t\t\t<artifactId>japicmp-test-v1</artifactId>\n\t\t\t<version>${project.version}</version>\n\t\t</dependency>\n\t\t<dependency>\n\t\t\t<groupId>com.github.siom79.japicmp</groupId>\n\t\t\t<artifactId>japicmp-test2-v1</artifactId>\n\t\t\t<version>${project.version}</version>\n\t\t</dependency>\n\t</oldVersions>\n\t<newVersions>\n\t\t<dependency>\n\t\t\t<groupId>com.github.siom79.japicmp</groupId>\n\t\t\t<artifactId>japicmp-test-v2</artifactId>\n\t\t\t<version>${project.version}</version>\n\t\t</dependency>\n\t\t<dependency>\n\t\t\t<groupId>com.github.siom79.japicmp</groupId>\n\t\t\t<artifactId>japicmp-test2-v2</artifactId>\n\t\t\t<version>${project.version}</version>\n\t\t</dependency>\n\t</newVersions>\n\t...\n</configuration>\n```\n\nThe configuration above will create one report for all the declared dependencies.\n\n## Using Groovy scripts for post analysis\n\nThe parameter <postAnalysisScript/> can be used to invoke a [Groovy](http://www.groovy-lang.org/) script after the analysis but before the output is written. This is helpful if you want to apply some custom filtering that is not possible with the standard means of japicmp. The following script for example filters out all classes that reside within the package `japicmp.test.annotation` and all methods that start with `get...()` and `set...()`.\n\n```groovy\ndef it = jApiClasses.iterator()\nwhile (it.hasNext()) {\n\tdef jApiClass = it.next()\n\tdef fqn = jApiClass.getFullyQualifiedName()\n\tif (fqn.startsWith(\"japicmp.test.annotation\")) {\n\t\tit.remove()\n\t}\n\tdef methodIt = jApiClass.getMethods().iterator()\n\twhile (methodIt.hasNext()) {\n\t\tdef method = methodIt.next()\n\t\tif (method.getName().startsWith(\"get\") || method.getName().startsWith(\"set\")) {\n\t\t\tmethodIt.remove()\n\t\t}\n\t}\n}\nreturn jApiClasses\n```\n\nPlease note that the script has to return a list of `JApiClass` objects, otherwise the maven plugin will report an error.\n\nBeyond that the script can also be used to break the build on some project specific requirement. Let's assume that for the next release no classes within the package `japicmp.test.annotation` should be modified in any way. The following Groovy script iterates over all classes and throws an exception if a class is not `UNCHANGED`.\n\n```groovy\nimport static japicmp.model.JApiChangeStatus.*\n\ndef it = jApiClasses.iterator()\nwhile (it.hasNext()) {\n\tdef jApiClass = it.next()\n\tdef fqn = jApiClass.getFullyQualifiedName()\n\tif (fqn.startsWith(\"japicmp.test.annotation\")) {\n\t\tif (jApiClass.getChangeStatus() != UNCHANGED) {\n\t\t\tthrow new Exception(\"Class in package japicmp.test.annotation has been modified.\")\n\t\t}\n\t}\n}\nreturn jApiClasses\n```\n\nThe script can also be placed inside another artifact and loaded from the classpath. Therefore, add the artifact containing the script as dependency to the japicmp-plugin:\n\n```xml\n<plugin>\n\t<groupId>com.github.siom79.japicmp</groupId>\n\t<artifactId>japicmp-maven-plugin</artifactId>\n\t<version>${project.version}</version>\n\t<configuration>\n\t\t[...]\n\t\t<parameter>\n\t\t\t<!-- The post-analysis script is contained in \"post-analysis-script-artifact\" -->\n\t\t\t<postAnalysisScript>post-analysis-script.groovy</postAnalysisScript>\n\t\t</parameter>\n\t</configuration>\n\t<dependencies>\n\t\t<dependency>\n\t\t\t<groupId>com.github.siom79.japicmp</groupId>\n\t\t\t<artifactId>post-analysis-script-artifact</artifactId>\n\t\t\t<version>${project.version}</version>\n\t\t</dependency>\n\t</dependencies>\n</plugin>\n```\n\n---\n\n© 2026","metadata":{"truncated":false},"title":"https://siom79.github.io/japicmp/MavenPlugin.html (text/html; charset=utf-8)","time":{"start":1782061521750,"end":1782061522534}},"metadata":{"openai":{"itemId":"fc_034271ef5e0ed583016a3819d12db48190a0171f0216fea58a"}},"id":"prt_eeb24da03001SCdefflbVoxNgY","sessionID":"ses_114e587ccffeR7j7jaWoRjsepW","messageID":"msg_eeb24c3ca001g0BWVJ546z3QNY"}}
